Use when the user says "review for over-engineering", "what can we delete", "is this over-engineered", "simplify review", or invokes /lazy-review. Complements correctness-focused review, this one only hunts complexity. ([AaronAbuUsama/code-factory](https://github.com/AaronAbuUsama/code-factory/tree/HEAD/skills/lazy-review))
- **lazy-review**: Review the current working diff for over-engineering against the YAGNI ladder and return a structured delete-list — specific lines/files to remove or collapse, each tagged with the rung it failed. Read-only: it proposes deletions, it does not apply them. Use when the user says "lazy review", "review my diff for over-engineering", "is this over-built", "what can I delete", or invokes /lazy-review. Unlike the built-in /simplify (which applies fixes) or /review-pr (correctness/coverage), this only proposes a delete-list. Never flags the safety set — trust-boundary validation, data-loss handling,… ([JasonFulksFamilySearch/claude-os](https://github.com/JasonFulksFamilySearch/claude-os/tree/HEAD/skills/lazy-review))
- **lazy-review.audit**: Run when the operator asks whether the review setup is sane, or when review misbehaves in a way that smells like config — a document never enters the loop, a class points at an expert that was never registered, commits land under the wrong identity. Read-only; fixes come from `/lazy-review.configure` or `/lazy-review.install`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.audit) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.configure**: Run when the operator wants a new kind of document to go through review, wants to change which paths a review class matches, or wants to reassign who writes / validates / closes out / narrates it. Requires `/lazy-review.install` to have run first. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.configure) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.finalize**: Use when a document is already approved but still looks like a review artefact — banner, approve checkbox, edit markers, system callouts — and the operator wants it closed out by hand: 'finalize this', 'clean up the review markup', or the automatic finalize never fired. Not for an unapproved doc — that is `/lazy-review.stop`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.finalize) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.install**: Run when the operator asks to set up document review in this repo, after a lazycortex-review update, or when review skills fail because `lazy.settings.json` has no `review` section, the `.experts/.jobs/` queue is missing, or the coordinator routines were never registered. Per-repo bootstrap only — wiring the first document class is `/lazy-review.configure`. Idempotent and quiet on re-run. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.install) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.start**: Use when a markdown document is NOT yet in the review loop and the operator wants it to be — 'put this under review', 'start the review on X', or resuming a document `/lazy-review.stop` parked. A fresh review opens at the writing round; use `/lazy-review.submit` instead when the content is already written and only needs reviewing. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.start) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.status**: Use when the operator asks where a document stands in the review — is it still active, which round it is on, whether it is approved, what the banner is currently waiting for, which expert owns which section. Also the cheap way to check state before deciding between `/lazy-review.start`, `/lazy-review.stop`, and `/lazy-review.finalize`. Read-only; emits one line of JSON. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.status) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.stop**: Use when a document is currently in the review loop and the operator wants the experts to stop touching it before it is approved — 'pause the review', 'take this out of review for now', or a churning doc that needs parking. `/lazy-review.start` resumes it; for a document that IS approved and just needs closing out, use `/lazy-review.finalize`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.stop) · ★1)
- **lazy-review.submit**: Use when a document is NOT yet in the review loop, its content is already written, and the operator wants it reviewed rather than drafted — 'submit this for review', 'I've made the edits, get it reviewed'. Use `/lazy-review.start` instead when the experts should write the document first. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-review/skills/lazy-review.submit) · ★1)
- **lazy-routine.offer-protocols**: Run when the operator asks to attach optional protocol references to a writer-dispatching routine — it discovers the flagged candidates, offers the contextually relevant ones, and unions the picks into the routine's flat `protocols` list. Operator-invoked only: install skills never dispatch it, a system routine's protocol set is fixed by design and seeded without questions. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-routine.offer-protocols) · ★1)
- **lazy-routine.register**: Run when the daemon should start doing something on its own — the operator asks to schedule recurring work, watch an inbox directory, react to local git HEAD, or scan markdown files by frontmatter. Also dispatched by plugin install skills to wire their own routines instead of hand-writing settings JSON. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-routine.register) · ★1)
- **lazy-routine.unregister**: Run when the operator asks to stop a daemon routine for good, or before re-registering one with a different shape (register refuses to overwrite). Idempotent on a name that is not registered; refuses to remove the built-in `lazy-expert.pump` without `--force`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-routine.unregister) · ★1)
- **lazy-runtime.preflight**: Run before wiring a new expert or MCP server into a live routine, and when a routine's expert spawns keep timing out, die instantly, or never produce a response. Emulates each expert launch with a trivial prompt and proposes a fix, applied only after the operator confirms. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-runtime.preflight) · ★1)
- **lazy-runtime.recover**: Run when the runtime daemon has stopped scheduling — routines no longer fire, or `.runtime/state.json` carries a `daemon_halted` block — and the operator asks to unblock it. Walks the operator through the halt reason, then clears the halt so the daemon resumes. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-runtime.recover) · ★1)
- **lazy-runtime.tick**: Run when the operator asks to tick the runtime by hand — run the due routines once, drain the queues, or fire one named routine — on a checkout whose daemon is not running. Same primitives and serial order as the daemon; commits like the daemon, never pushes without an explicit word. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-core/skills/lazy-runtime.tick) · ★1)
- **lazy-scrum-team**: All-in-one AI Virtual Scrum Team covering the full sprint lifecycle: PO, Backlog Review, Architect, Architecture Review, Scrum Master, MR Plan Review, Developer, Code Review, Test Engineer, Final Acceptance, Daily Standup, and Sprint Retrospective. Routes to the correct role, enforces approval gates, and handles rework loops. Use when the user mentions virtual scrum team, AI scrum team, sprint planning, backlog, architecture design, task splitting, MR planning, coding a task, code review, testing, final acceptance, daily standup, retrospective, or any Scrum role activity. - **lazy-sections**: The lazy-sections pattern for vertically scrolling, backend-driven feeds of heterogeneous content — one outer lazy container, typed section kinds, immutable cell values, and scroll-performance rules. ([synchromation/SecretDJ](https://github.com/synchromation/SecretDJ/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lazy-sections))
- **lazy-senior**: Writes the smallest complete change: asks if it needs to exist, then reuses the codebase, standard library or an installed dependency. - **lazy-senior**: Write the smallest, most boring solution that actually ships — reuse before writing, stdlib/native before dependencies, one line when one line works — without ever cutting validation, security, error handling, accessibility, or tests. Use when the user says "keep it simple", "don't over-engineer", "minimal change", "ponytail this", or when a change is drifting toward needless abstraction, a new dependency, or speculative flexibility. ([MalloyTheDev/awesomeplugin](https://github.com/MalloyTheDev/awesomeplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/awesomeplugin/skills/lazy-senior))
- **lazy-senior**: Выбирает наименьшее полное изменение и называет, что не проверено. Лень не распространяется на чтение и безопасность. ([mazurovn/BMAD_BDD_SDD_ADR_SKILL_TOOLS](https://github.com/mazurovn/BMAD_BDD_SDD_ADR_SKILL_TOOLS/tree/HEAD/skills/lazy-senior))
- **lazy-senior**: Use before writing any feature or implementation code. Enforces the smallest correct change - fewest lines, reuse before build, no speculative abstraction. ([studio-senkou/senkou-lentera-cendekia-dashboard-fe](https://github.com/studio-senkou/senkou-lentera-cendekia-dashboard-fe/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/lazy-senior))
- **lazy-senior-dev**: Use when writing, refactoring, or fixing code, and when the user says "be lazy", "minimal solution", "YAGNI", or complains about over-engineering or bloat. Reuse before write, stdlib before dependency, the minimal version of the agreed design. ([MudraMartin/dotlight-skillset](https://github.com/MudraMartin/dotlight-skillset/tree/HEAD/superpowers/lazy-senior-dev))
- **lazy-senior-dev**: The lazy senior developer review - efficiency as deliberate laziness, not carelessness. Climb the ladder (does this need to exist? does it already exist? is it one line?) - **lazy-senior-engineer**: Acts as a "smart-lazy" senior software engineer who masters the repo before touching it and ships zero-regression, maintainable solutions with the minimum necessary code. Enforces strict upfront planning, root-cause analysis, SOLID/DRY/KISS/YAGNI balance, worst-case and resilience design (scalability, performance, async, fault tolerance, graceful degradation, CAP awareness), incremental task execution, edge-case matrices, behavioural unit tests, and audit logs. - **lazy-skill-drop**: Use when the user wants help bringing a new Claude skill to life — says "I want to build a skill that [does X]", "我想开发一个 [X] 技能", "I want to develop a [name] skill", "我想做一个能 [X] 的 skill", "帮我设计一个 skill", "I'm building a skill for [purpose]", "/lazydrop", or otherwise wants competitor recon, a differentiated design, viral-formula docs, and one-confirm GitHub release. - **lazy-spec.add-asset-type**: Use when a product must grow a new kind of asset beyond the shipped feature / change / bug / content / research / release set — characters, scenes, chapters, endpoints, whatever the operator names — or when `lazy-spec.request-classify` has no type to route a request into. - **lazy-spec.audit**: Use when checking a product spec for staleness, broken links, missing sections, role/header violations, or drift from the source code — 'audit this spec', 'is the spec tree healthy'. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.audit) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.coverage**: Run when the operator asks to find capabilities the code already has that the product's spec tree does not cover yet — 'what's missing from the specs', 'gap-scan this product against its code'. - **lazy-spec.create-asset**: Use when the user asks to add a new feature, change, bug — or any operator-declared asset type such as characters / scenes / chapters — to a product that already has a spec. The built-in `lazy-spec.create-feature` / `lazy-spec.create-change` / `lazy-spec.create-bug` skills only pin the type and delegate here; invoke this one directly for every other type. - **lazy-spec.create-bug**: Use when filing a bug against a product spec. Which document the asset starts from is declared by the type declaration (`asset_types.bug.start_doc`) and read by the delegate, never by this wrapper. - **lazy-spec.create-change**: Use when requesting a change to an existing product spec — the atomic modification unit, peer to a feature. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.create-change) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.create-feature**: Use when adding a new feature to a product that already has a spec. Built-in wrapper: pins `&lt;asset-type&gt;` to `feature` and delegates — all clarification, scaffolding, prose, and diagrams are owned by `lazy-spec.create-asset`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.create-feature) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.create-from-code**: Use when the operator asks to write a spec FROM existing code — 'spec this codebase', 'document this product from its source', 'spec this feature from the code' — for a product already registered with a `source` binding via /lazy-spec.product-config. Pick it over /lazy-spec.sync-with-code when no spec exists yet; sync updates one that does. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.create-from-code) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.create-request**: Use when the user has a raw idea, complaint, or ask they want written down into the vault's `requests/` inbox rather than acted on now — 'note this down', 'file a request for X', 'I want this somewhere so it does not get lost'. Runs a 3-5 question wizard first, then writes a body-only file; the request-handling routines add frontmatter and route it from there. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.create-request) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.drive**: Use when the operator wants to drive one spec asset through its ladder by hand, in one session, with no runtime daemon acting on this checkout — `/lazy-spec.drive &lt;asset-note-path&gt;`. It performs each gesture only on the operator's word, never as its own decision, and refuses to start while a live daemon could act on the same checkout. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.drive) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.flip-gate**: Run when the operator declares an asset's progression gate reached or wants one walked back — 'design is approved', 'tests pass now', 'this is released', 'un-flip spec_released'. Interactive by default (one confirm question); non-interactive callers such as `spec.coordinator` pass `--auto`. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.flip-gate) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.gate-tick**: Dispatched per status folder-note by the daemon's `lazy-spec.gate-tick` md-scan routine; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.gate-tick) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.install**: Run when the operator asks to set up the spec system in a repo, after enabling or updating lazycortex-specs, or when spec skills misbehave because the `spec` settings section, the `lazy-spec.gate-tick` routine, or the vault spec (`vision.md`) is missing. - **lazy-spec.lookup**: Use when an agent doing research over the spec tree, or the operator asking where something lives in specs or what depends on it, needs a bounded slice around a product or asset — a question or token plus an optional anchor, returning matching paths with short excerpts, never whole documents. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.lookup) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.product-config**: Use when the operator asks to create a new product in the spec system or edit an existing product's registration — 'add a product', 'register this repo as a product', 'add source to this design-only product', 'change its dependencies, language or icon'. Products are registered here, never by /lazy-spec.install. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.product-config) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.record-decision**: Use when the operator wants to record, supersede, obsolete, or promote an entry in the spec catalog's decisions registry — 'log this decision', 'mark D-007 superseded', 'D-012 is obsolete now', 'pull the decision blocks out of design.md'. Interactive wrapper over the `decide` CLI primitive; never edits a `decisions.md` file by hand. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.record-decision) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.refresh-sources**: Use after hand-editing a spec doc's `spec_source_docs` / `spec_source_requests` frontmatter, or whenever its body `# Sources` bullets no longer match that frontmatter. Re-projects the `## Docs` / `## Requests` lists, keeping operator glosses on existing wikilinks. Touches nothing outside the doc's `# Sources` container. One file per call — callers loop. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.refresh-sources) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.request-classify**: Dispatched by the `spec.catalog-coordinator` agent in routing mode to label a request with a `request_class`; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.request-classify) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.request-find-candidates**: Dispatched by the `spec.catalog-coordinator` agent in routing mode to find assets a request could attach to; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.request-find-candidates) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.resolve-dependency**: Dispatched by /lazy-spec.product-config to classify or link one `dependencies[]` entry; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.resolve-dependency) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.set-stage**: Use when one authored spec doc changes stage — a draft is approved, a plan is rejected, an asset is cancelled. Accepts any document whose `spec_doc_type` is declared with `stages: true`, never a filename or a path. Every `spec.*` skill that moves a per-file `spec_stage` delegates here instead of editing frontmatter, so the `spec/&lt;stage&gt;` mirror tag and the `spec_approved_at` stamp never drift. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.set-stage) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.source-url**: Dispatched by every `spec.*` skill and agent that emits a link to a file of the product's code; not for direct use. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.source-url) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.sync-with-code**: Use when source code has changed and the spec tree should match it again — 'sync the specs with the code', 'the code moved on, update the spec' — for the whole tree, one code-bound product, or one asset. Every edit stays uncommitted until the operator reads the diff and says yes. Pick /lazy-spec.create-from-code instead when no spec exists yet. ([mebius-san/lazy-cortex](https://github.com/mebius-san/lazy-cortex/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/lazycortex-specs/skills/lazy-spec.sync-with-code) · ★1)
- **lazy-spec.upstream-run**: Run when the operator asks to fetch upstream sources now, check what changed in a configured `spec.upstream` source, or force a mirror/detect pass without waiting for the next `lazy-spec.upstream-tick` schedule tick. Manual, no-daemon counterpart of that routine — same underlying primitive, same result.
